DESIGN.md

show
## Concept
The player guides a glowing dot through an ever‑flowing tunnel, tapping to generate a short‑lived bubble that slows every obstacle inside it, letting the dot slip through densely packed sections before the bubble fades and must recharge.

## Player
- **Avatar**: a simple, luminous dot that continuously moves forward through the tunnel.
- **Actions**: 
  - *Move*: subtle lateral shifts to align with gaps (left/right or mouse drag).
  - *Create bubble*: tap / click (or press a dedicated key) to spawn a temporary slowing field around the dot.
  - *Restart*: press a key or click the restart button to begin again.
The player never directly controls obstacle speed; they only manage timing of the bubble.

## Core loop
Every few seconds the player scans the upcoming passage, sees a cluster of obstacles, and decides whether to deploy the bubble. If the bubble is active, all obstacles inside it move slower, creating a brief window to thread through. The bubble then fades, entering a short recharge period during which the player must rely on precise movement. Obstacles continuously scroll forward, the score ticks upward with each cleared segment, and the tension of bubble timing drives the moment‑to‑moment gameplay.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Sweeping shards**: fast horizontal elements that cut across the tunnel, leaving narrow safe lanes.
- **Closing walls**: pairs of walls that slide inward to narrow the passage, then retreat.
- **Rotating blades**: radial obstacles that spin around a fixed point, sweeping any space they occupy.
- **Particle storms**: dense clouds that drift forward, preventing progress unless slowed by the bubble.
All hazards move with the tunnel’s base speed; when inside the bubble they are temporarily slowed, giving the player a brief reprieve.

## Score
- *Segment cleared*: each time the player passes a predefined checkpoint without collision.
- *Bubble success*: using a bubble to survive a dense cluster awards a bonus.
- *Pickup collected*: occasional time‑extension or score token that appears only when the bubble is active.
Score is displayed continuously and only changes on these discrete events.

## Win and lose
- **Lose**: the dot collides with any obstacle while the bubble is inactive (or the bubble has expired and the obstacle catches the player). The screen shows a Game Over overlay with final score and restart prompt.
- **Win**: survive through a set number of segments or reach a distant “safe zone” at the tunnel’s end, triggering a celebratory overlay and final score.

## Feel
Frantic, neon‑lit tunnel racing with a pulse‑driven synth soundtrack. The aesthetic is high‑contrast neon on a dark background, with glowing trails and subtle particle effects. Audio cues punctuate bubble activation, obstacle proximity, and scoring events, reinforcing the tense, fast‑paced experience.

## Why it's fun
The exhilaration of timing a fleeting bubble to carve a path through a relentless gauntlet creates a crisp, satisfying moment of control and relief.

TASKS.md

show
1. HTML scaffold & meta.json — create index.html with header, canvas, restart button and stub script; create meta.json matching header and controls.
2. Game state objects — define plain objects for player, hazards array, score, and phase at top of script.
3. Animation loop — implement startLoop() that cancels previous loop, uses requestAnimationFrame, clears canvas each frame, wraps update/render calls in try/catch.
4. Input handling — set up a Set `keys`, attach keydown/keyup listeners, map R key and restart button to a reset() function.
5. Player movement — apply dt‑scaled acceleration and friction based on arrow keys, clamp to canvas bounds, update player position each frame.
6. Hazard system — create hazard objects, spawn them respecting SAFE_RADIUS from player at t = 0, move them with dt‑scaled velocity.
7. Collision detection — check player vs hazards each frame; on hit set phase to "gameover" and stop gameplay.
8. Scoring — increase score on each survived hazard or time event, render numeric score at top‑left of canvas.
9. Rendering — clear canvas, draw background, draw each hazard, draw player as highest‑contrast shape, draw HUD elements.
10. Game‑over overlay — dim canvas, draw "Game Over" with final score and restart instructions.
11. Observable contract — populate `window.__game` each frame with state, score, player position/visibility, and a one‑sentence objective.
12. Audio feedback — lazily create an AudioContext in a beep() helper, wrap calls in try/catch and trigger sounds on hit, score, and death.

<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Time Bubble</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Create a short-lived bubble that slows nearby obstacles, letting you slip through dense sections of the tunnel while the bubble fades and recharges.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Tap to create bubble</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  // Configuration constants
  const W = 800;
  const H = 600;
  const PLAYER_RADIUS = 5;
  const PLAYER_THRUST = 300; // px/sec^2
  const PLAYER_FRICTION = 0.8; // per second
  const HAZARD_RADIUS = 12;
  const HAZARD_SPEED = 100; // px/sec
  const SPAWN_INTERVAL = 2; // sec
  const SAFE_RADIUS = 100;
  const BUBBLE_RADIUS = 80;
  const BUBBLE_DURATION = 0.6; // sec
  const BUBBLE_COOLDOWN = 3; // sec

  const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  // Game state objects
  let player = { x: 400, y: 300, vx: 0, vy: 0, angle: 0, alive: true };
  let hazards = [];
  let score = 0;
  let phase = "menu"; // "menu" | "playing" | "gameover"
  let keys; // Set of pressed keys, initialized on reset
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    player = { x: 400, y: 300, vx: 0, vy: 0, angle: 0, alive: true };
    hazards = [];
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    startLoop();
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      // TODO: update and render logic will go here
      requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", ev => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    if (keys) keys.add(ev.key);
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", ev => {
    if (keys) keys.delete(ev.key);
  });
  document.getElementById("restart").addEventListener("click", reset);

  // Start the game immediately
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

Add beep function:

```
function beep(freq, dur) {
    try {
        if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
        osc.type = "sine";
        osc.frequency.value = freq;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime+dur);
        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
        osc.start();
        osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime+dur);
    } catch(e) {}
}
```

Call beep(440,0.1) on hazard hit (death). Call beep(880,0.05) on score tick maybe.

We can modify startLoop to:

```
function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      update(dt);
      render();
      publishState();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('loop error', err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}
```

In tick after try/catch we set loopId.

Specifically, oldString: `"  // === INPUT =============================================================="` (including spaces). We'll replace with:

```
  // === UPDATE ==============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;
    // Player movement
    const left = keys.has("ArrowLeft") || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A");
    const right = keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D");
    const up = keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W");
    const down = keys.has("ArrowDown") || keys.has("s") || keys.has("S");
    if (left) player.vx -= PLAYER_THRUST * dt;
    if (right) player.vx += PLAYER_THRUST * dt;
    if (up) player.vy -= PLAYER_THRUST * dt;
    if (down) player.vy += PLAYER_THRUST * dt;
    // Friction
    const decay = Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);
    player.vx *= decay;
    player.vy *= decay;
    // Position update
    player.x += player.vx * dt;
    player.y += player.vy * dt;
    // Clamp to canvas
    if (player.x < PLAYER_RADIUS) player.x = PLAYER_RADIUS;
    if (player.x > W - PLAYER_RADIUS) player.x = W - PLAYER_RADIUS;
    if (player.y < PLAYER_RADIUS) player.y = PLAYER_RADIUS;
    if (player.y > H - PLAYER_RADIUS) player.y = H - PLAYER_RADIUS;
    // Hazard spawn timer
    spawnTimer -= dt;
    if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
      spawnHazard();
      spawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL;
    }
    // Update hazards
    for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const h = hazards[i];
      h.x += h.vx * dt;
      h.y += h.vy * dt;
      // Remove off-screen hazards
      if (h.x < -HAZARD_RADIUS || h.x > W + HAZARD_RADIUS || h.y < -HAZARD_RADIUS || h.y > H + HAZARD_RADIUS) {
        hazards.splice(i, 1);
        continue;
      }
      // Bubble effect
      if (bubble) {
        const dx = h.x - bubble.x;
        const dy = h.y - bubble.y;
        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        if (dist < bubble.radius) {
          h.vx *= 0.3;
          h.vy *= 0.3;
        }
      }
      // Collision with player
      const distP = Math.hypot(h.x - player.x, h.y - player.y);
      if (distP < PLAYER_RADIUS + h.r) {
        phase = "gameover";
        beep(200, 0.5);
        break;
      }
    }
    // Update bubble timer
    if (bubble) {
      bubble.time += dt;
      if (bubble.time >= bubble.duration) {
        bubble = null;
        bubbleCooldown = BUBBLE_COOLDOWN;
      }
    } else {
      if (bubbleCooldown > 0) {
        bubbleCooldown -= dt;
      }
    }
    // Score over time
    score += dt * 10;
  }

  // === RENDER ==============================================================
  function render() {
    // Clear
    ctx.fillStyle = "#05050c";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    // Hazards
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ff00aa";
    for (const h of hazards) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(h.x, h.y, h.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    // Player
    ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, PLAYER_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    // Bubble
    if (bubble) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = "#ffd23f";
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(bubble.x, bubble.y, bubble.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    // HUD
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText("Score " + Math.floor(score), 12, 22);
    // Game Over overlay
    if (phase === "gameover") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(5,5,12,0.65)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Game Over", W / 2, H / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Final score " + Math.floor(score), W / 2, H / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", W / 2, H / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
```

```
  document.getElementById("restart").addEventListener("click", reset);
  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, dur) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "sine";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + dur);
      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start();
      osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + dur);
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ================================================
  function publishState() {
    const visible = player.x >= 0 && player.x < W && player.y >= 0 && player.y < H;
    window.__game = {
      state: phase,
      score: Math.floor(score),
      player: {
        x: player.x,
        y: player.y,
        visible,
      },
      objective: "Create bubbles to slow obstacles and survive.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function spawnHazard() {
    // spawn hazard at right edge, random y, respecting safe radius from player at spawn
    let x = W + HAZARD_RADIUS;
    let y = Math.random() * (H - HAZARD_RADIUS * 2) + HAZARD_RADIUS;
    // ensure safe distance from player
    const dx = x - player.x;
    const dy = y - player.y;
    if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) < SAFE_RADIUS) {
      // place on left side alternative
      x = -HAZARD_RADIUS;
    }
    hazards.push({ x, y, vx: -HAZARD_SPEED, vy: 0, r: HAZARD_RADIUS });
  }
```

Note we also need to place the `// === HELPERS` after observable contract (or before) but it's okay.

  // Tap to create bubble
  canvas.addEventListener("click", () => {
    if (bubble || bubbleCooldown > 0) return;

We'll replace with:\n\n```\n  // === UPDATE ==============================================================\n  function update(dt) {\n    if (phase !== \"playing\") return;\n    // Player movement\n    const left = keys.has(\"ArrowLeft\") || keys.has(\"a\") || keys.has(\"A\");\n    const right = keys.has(\"ArrowRight\") || keys.has(\"d\") || keys.has(\"D\");\n    const up = keys.has(\"ArrowUp\") || keys.has(\"w\") || keys.has(\"W\");\n    const down = keys.has(\"ArrowDown\") || keys.has(\"s\") || keys.has(\"S\");\n    if (left) player.vx -= PLAYER_THRUST * dt;\n    if (right) player.vx += PLAYER_THRUST * dt;\n    if (up) player.vy -= PLAYER_THRUST * dt;\n    if (down) player.vy += PLAYER_THRUST * dt;\n    // Friction\n    const decay = Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);\n    player.vx *= decay;\n    player.vy *= decay;\n    // Position update\n    player.x += player.vx * dt;\n    player.y += player.vy * dt;\n    // Clamp to canvas\n    if (player.x < PLAYER_RADIUS) player.x = PLAYER_RADIUS;\n    if (player.x > W - PLAYER_RADIUS) player.x = W - PLAYER_RADIUS;\n    if (player.y < PLAYER_RADIUS) player.y = PLAYER_RADIUS;\n    if (player.y > H - PLAYER_RADIUS) player.y = H - PLAYER_RADIUS;\n    // Hazard spawn timer\n    spawnTimer -= dt;\n    if (spawnTimer <= 0) {\n      spawnHazard();\n      spawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL;\n    }\n    // Update hazards\n    for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n      const h = hazards[i];\n      h.x += h.vx * dt;\n      h.y += h.vy * dt;\n      // Remove off-screen hazards\n      if (h.x < -HAZARD_RADIUS || h.x > W + HAZARD_RADIUS || h.y < -HAZARD_RADIUS || h.y > H + HAZARD_RADIUS) {\n        hazards.splice(i, 1);\n        continue;\n      }\n      // Bubble effect\n      if (bubble) {\n        const dx = h.x - bubble.x;\n        const dy = h.y - bubble.y;\n        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n        if (dist < bubble.radius) {\n          h.vx *= 0.3;\n          h.vy *= 0.3;\n        }\n      }\n      // Collision with player\n      const distP = Math.hypot(h.x - player.x, h.y - player.y);\n      if (distP < PLAYER_RADIUS + h.r) {\n        phase = \"gameover\";\n        beep(200, 0.5);\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n    // Update bubble timer\n    if (bubble) {\n      bubble.time += dt;\n      if (bubble.time >= bubble.duration) {\n        bubble = null;\n        bubbleCooldown = BUBBLE_COOLDOWN;\n      }\n    } else {\n      if (bubbleCooldown > 0) {\n        bubbleCooldown -= dt;\n      }\n    }\n    // Score over time\n    score += dt * 10;\n  }\n\n  // === RENDER ==============================================================\n  function render() {\n    // Clear\n    ctx.fillStyle = \"#05050c\";\n    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);\n    // Hazards\n    ctx.fillStyle = \"#ff00aa\";\n    for (const h of hazards) {\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(h.x, h.y, h.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n      ctx.fill();\n    }\n    // Player\n    ctx.fillStyle = \"#7df9ff\";\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, PLAYER_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n    ctx.fill();\n    // Bubble\n    if (bubble) {\n      ctx.strokeStyle = \"#ffd23f\";\n      ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(bubble.x, bubble.y, bubble.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n      ctx.stroke();\n    }\n    // HUD\n    ctx.fillStyle = \"#e6e6f0\";\n    ctx.font = \"16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui\";\n    ctx.textAlign = \"left\";\n    ctx.fillText(\"Score \" + Math.floor(score), 12, 22);\n    // Game Over overlay\n    if (phase === \"gameover\") {\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(5,5,12,0.65)\";\n      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"#ff52ae\";\n      ctx.textAlign = \"center\";\n      ctx.font = \"44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui\";\n      ctx.fillText(\"Game Over\", W / 2, H / 2 - 20);\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"#e6e6f0\";\n      ctx.font = \"20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui\";\n      ctx.fillText(\"Final score \" + Math.floor(score), W / 2, H / 2 + 14);\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"#a5a5b8\";\n      ctx.font = \"16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui\";\n      ctx.fillText(\"Press R or click Restart\", W / 2, H / 2 + 42);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // === INPUT ==============================================================\n```\n\nThus replacing the input comment line with the above block that includes update, render, then input comment.\n\nThat will preserve the input section code after it. prompt (15,969 chars)
## Reference example: "asteroids"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

### Example `meta.json`
```json
{
  "title": "Neon Asteroids",
  "description": "Rotate, thrust, and shoot. Big rocks split into small ones. Get hit and you're gone.",
  "controls": [
    "Left/Right (or A/D) to rotate",
    "Up (or W) to thrust",
    "Space to fire",
    "Press R or click Restart"
  ],
  "tags": ["arcade", "shooter", "physics"]
}
```

### Example `index.html`
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Neon Asteroids</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #05050c; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Asteroids</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Rotate, thrust, and shoot. Big rocks split into small ones. Get hit and you're gone.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Left/Right (or A/D) to rotate</li>
      <li>Up (or W) to thrust</li>
      <li>Space to fire</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const W = 800, H = 600;
  const SHIP_RADIUS = 12;
  const SHIP_THRUST = 280;       // px/sec^2
  const SHIP_FRICTION = 0.6;     // velocity decay per second (1 = none)
  const SHIP_TURN = Math.PI * 1.8; // rad/sec
  const BULLET_SPEED = 520;      // px/sec
  const BULLET_LIFE = 1.0;       // sec
  const FIRE_COOLDOWN = 0.20;    // sec between shots
  const ASTEROID_BIG_R = 38;
  const ASTEROID_MID_R = 22;
  const ASTEROID_LIL_R = 12;
  const ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE = [40, 110];
  const SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN = 160; // no asteroid spawns inside this disk

  const SCORE_BIG = 20, SCORE_MID = 50, SCORE_LIL = 100;

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let ship;        // { x, y, vx, vy, angle, alive }
  let bullets;     // [{ x, y, vx, vy, life }]
  let asteroids;   // [{ x, y, vx, vy, r, shape: number[] }]
  let score;
  let highScore = 0;
  let phase;       // "playing" | "gameover"
  let fireCooldown;
  let keys;
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    ship = { x: W / 2, y: H / 2, vx: 0, vy: 0, angle: -Math.PI / 2, alive: true };
    bullets = [];
    asteroids = [];
    spawnWave(5);
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    fireCooldown = 0;
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  function spawnWave(n) {
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) spawnAsteroid(ASTEROID_BIG_R);
  }

  function spawnAsteroid(r) {
    let x, y, tries = 0;
    do {
      x = Math.random() * W;
      y = Math.random() * H;
      tries++;
    } while (Math.hypot(x - ship.x, y - ship.y) < SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN && tries < 30);
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const speed = ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0] + Math.random() * (ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[1] - ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0]);
    asteroids.push({
      x, y,
      vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
      vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
      r,
      shape: makeAsteroidShape(),
    });
  }

  function makeAsteroidShape() {
    // Per-vertex radius multipliers, 8-12 vertices for a knobbly silhouette.
    const n = 8 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);
    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) out.push(0.75 + Math.random() * 0.45);
    return out;
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;

    // Ship.
    if (ship.alive) {
      const turnIn = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0)
                   - (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
      ship.angle += turnIn * SHIP_TURN * dt;

      const thrusting = keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W");
      if (thrusting) {
        ship.vx += Math.cos(ship.angle) * SHIP_THRUST * dt;
        ship.vy += Math.sin(ship.angle) * SHIP_THRUST * dt;
      }
      // Mild friction so the ship doesn't drift forever.
      const decay = Math.pow(SHIP_FRICTION, dt);
      ship.vx *= decay;
      ship.vy *= decay;
      ship.x = wrap(ship.x + ship.vx * dt, W);
      ship.y = wrap(ship.y + ship.vy * dt, H);

      fireCooldown -= dt;
      if ((keys.has(" ") || keys.has("Space")) && fireCooldown <= 0) {
        fire();
        fireCooldown = FIRE_COOLDOWN;
      }
    }

    // Bullets.
    for (const b of bullets) {
      b.life -= dt;
      b.x = wrap(b.x + b.vx * dt, W);
      b.y = wrap(b.y + b.vy * dt, H);
    }
    bullets = bullets.filter((b) => b.life > 0);

    // Asteroids.
    for (const a of asteroids) {
      a.x = wrap(a.x + a.vx * dt, W);
      a.y = wrap(a.y + a.vy * dt, H);
    }

    // Bullet-asteroid collisions.
    for (let i = asteroids.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const a = asteroids[i];
      let hit = false;
      for (let j = bullets.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
        const b = bullets[j];
        if (Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y) < a.r) {
          bullets.splice(j, 1);
          hit = true;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (hit) splitAsteroid(i);
    }

    // Ship-asteroid collisions.
    if (ship.alive) {
      for (const a of asteroids) {
        if (Math.hypot(a.x - ship.x, a.y - ship.y) < a.r + SHIP_RADIUS * 0.6) {
          ship.alive = false;
          phase = "gameover";
          if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
          beep(110, 0.3);
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    // Wave clear -> spawn next.
    if (asteroids.length === 0 && phase === "playing") {
      spawnWave(5);
    }
  }

  function fire() {
    bullets.push({
      x: ship.x + Math.cos(ship.angle) * SHIP_RADIUS,
      y: ship.y + Math.sin(ship.angle) * SHIP_RADIUS,
      vx: ship.vx + Math.cos(ship.angle) * BULLET_SPEED,
      vy: ship.vy + Math.sin(ship.angle) * BULLET_SPEED,
      life: BULLET_LIFE,
    });
    beep(820, 0.04);
  }

  function splitAsteroid(idx) {
    const a = asteroids[idx];
    asteroids.splice(idx, 1);
    if (a.r === ASTEROID_BIG_R) {
      score += SCORE_BIG;
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) addChild(a, ASTEROID_MID_R);
      beep(440, 0.06);
    } else if (a.r === ASTEROID_MID_R) {
      score += SCORE_MID;
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) addChild(a, ASTEROID_LIL_R);
      beep(560, 0.05);
    } else {
      score += SCORE_LIL;
      beep(700, 0.05);
    }
  }

  function addChild(parent, r) {
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const speed = ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0] + Math.random() * (ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[1] - ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0]);
    asteroids.push({
      x: parent.x, y: parent.y,
      vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
      vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
      r,
      shape: makeAsteroidShape(),
    });
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#05050c";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    // Asteroids.
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#a5a5b8";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    for (const a of asteroids) drawAsteroid(a);

    // Bullets.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffd23f";
    for (const b of bullets) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, 2.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }

    // Ship.
    if (ship.alive) drawShip();

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Best ${highScore}`, W - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "gameover") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(5,5,12,0.65)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText("Game Over", W / 2, H / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, W / 2, H / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", W / 2, H / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  function drawShip() {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(ship.x, ship.y);
    ctx.rotate(ship.angle);
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(SHIP_RADIUS, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.9, SHIP_RADIUS * 0.7);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.5, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.9, -SHIP_RADIUS * 0.7);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.stroke();
    if (keys && (keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W"))) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = "#ffd23f";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.5, 0);
      ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS - 6 - Math.random() * 4, 0);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function drawAsteroid(a) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = 0; i < a.shape.length; i++) {
      const t = (i / a.shape.length) * Math.PI * 2;
      const r = a.r * a.shape[i];
      const x = a.x + Math.cos(t) * r;
      const y = a.y + Math.sin(t) * r;
      if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y); else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    }
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
    if (ev.key === " ") ev.preventDefault();
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "triangle";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase,                                  // "playing" | "gameover"
      score,
      player: {
        x: ship.x,
        y: ship.y,
        // wrap() keeps coords in [0, W) and [0, H), so visible is always true
        // — but assert it explicitly so the contract is honest.
        visible: ship.x >= 0 && ship.x < W && ship.y >= 0 && ship.y < H,
      },
      objective: "Shoot the asteroids before they hit your ship. Big rocks split into smaller ones.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function wrap(v, max) {
    if (v < 0) return v + max;
    if (v >= max) return v - max;
    return v;
  }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Time Bubble</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Create a short-lived bubble that slows nearby obstacles, letting you slip through dense sections of the tunnel. The bubble fades and recharges.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Tap to create bubble</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  // (empty for now)
  // === STATE ==============================================================
  // Game state objects
  let player = { x: 400, y: 300, vx: 0, vy: 0, angle: 0, alive: true };
  let hazards = []; // array of obstacles
  let score = 0;
  let phase = "menu"; // "menu" | "playing" | "gameover"
  // other variables for loop, input, audio etc.
  let keys; // placeholder for input set
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;
  // stub functions
  function reset() {
    // Initialize state
    player = { x: 400, y: 300, vx: 0, vy: 0, angle: 0, alive: true };
    hazards = [];
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    startLoop();
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      // placeholder update/render
      // ...
      requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  // Input handling (placeholder)
  document.addEventListener("keydown", ev => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", ev => {
    if (keys) keys.delete(ev.key);
  });
  document.getElementById("restart").addEventListener("click", reset);

  // Start the game
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  function tick(now) {
    const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
    lastTime = now;
    try {
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      // update and render if functions exist
      if (typeof update === 'function') update(dt);
      if (typeof render === 'function') render();
      // publishState if defined
      if (typeof publishState === 'function') publishState();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('loop error', err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

```js
function spawnHazard() {
    // spawn hazard with random position not within safe radius of player
    const maxAttempts = 30;
    let x, y;
    for (let i = 0; i < maxAttempts; i++) {
        x = Math.random() * W;
        y = Math.random() * H;
        if (Math.hypot(x - player.x, y - player.y) >= SAFE_RADIUS) break;
    }
    const speed = 100 + Math.random() * 100; // arbitrary
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    hazards.push({
        x, y,
        vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
        vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
        r: 12,
    });
}
function spawnHazards(n) {
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) spawnHazard();
}
```

Implementation of beep: 

function beep(freq, durSec) {
  try {
    if (!audioCtx) {
      audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    }
    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.type = "triangle";
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
  } catch (e) {
    // swallow errors (e.g., audio context not allowed)
    console.error("beep error", e);
  }
}

prompt (5,173 chars)
Read CONVENTIONS.md and DESIGN.md in your working directory before doing anything else.

Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Time Bubble
Concept: Tap creates a short-lived bubble around the dot that slows all obstacles inside it, letting you slip through dense sections while the rest of the tunnel moves at normal speed; the bubble then fades and recharges.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file.

**Tool calls MUST be emitted as actual function/tool invocations — NOT described or simulated inside your reasoning/analysis text.** Writing tool-shaped JSON like `{"filePath":"TASKS.md","content":"..."}` *inside your chain-of-thought* does NOT invoke any tool. It is just text in the analysis channel that nothing reads or executes. The same goes for `{"pattern":"**/*"}` etc. — those only do something when emitted as a real `tool_use` event from the action channel, never when written into your thinking. Several prior runs have failed by hallucinating tool calls inside reasoning; do not repeat that pattern. If at any point you "describe" a tool call in your thinking instead of invoking it, you must immediately invoke the real tool afterward. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

You may merge two trivial tasks if they truly belong together (e.g. tasks 1+2 if state is just three lines). You may split a complex task in two. Aim for 10–15 tasks total.

**Final check before you stop**: confirm you actually called your write/edit tool with `TASKS.md` as the path. If you only described the task list in chat without invoking the tool, the file will not exist on disk and validation will fail. After the file is written, stop. Do not begin implementation.

2. Game state objects — define plain objects for player, hazards array, score, and phase at top of script.
3. Animation loop — implement startLoop() that cancels previous loop, uses requestAnimationFrame, clears canvas each frame, wraps update/render calls in try/catch.
4. Input handling — set up a Set `keys`, attach keydown/keyup listeners, map R key and restart button to a reset() function.
5. Player movement — apply dt‑scaled acceleration and friction based on arrow keys, clamp to canvas bounds, update player position each frame.
6. Hazard system — create hazard objects, spawn them respecting SAFE_RADIUS from player at t = 0, move them with dt‑scaled velocity.
7. Collision detection — check player vs hazards each frame; on hit set phase to "gameover" and stop gameplay.
8. Scoring — increase score on each survived hazard or time event, render numeric score at top‑left of canvas.
9. Rendering — clear canvas, draw background, draw each hazard, draw player as highest‑contrast shape, draw HUD elements.
10. Game‑over overlay — dim canvas, draw "Game Over" with final score and restart instructions.
11. Observable contract — populate `window.__game` each frame with state, score, player position/visibility, and a one‑sentence objective.
12. Audio feedback — lazily create an AudioContext in a beep() helper, wrap calls in try/catch and trigger sounds on hit, score, and death.
